### Current Spec verification statement

diff/hash checks and non-overwriting evidence preservation. It did not import
project code or run syntax, fixture, Test, typecheck, build, package, launch,
GUI, provider or product commands. Therefore all new H/R/G/B/X results are
accurately `NOT RUN`; no planned assertion is reported PASS or RED by Spec.

The completed decision package now fixes:

- one real-source loading boundary for Main/preload and one exact TSX
  transpilation boundary for the same final Renderer, each with independent
  positive/negative health;
- exact B1 BRIDGE -> ENTRY -> SAFETY -> retained IPC ordering;
- exact B2 COMPONENT -> MOUNT -> tuple(2)/runner(9)/typecheck/package ordering;
- package manifest/aggregate and positive/negative GUI launch control;
- two applicable real-package runtime-safety leaves before the five existing
  AC001 leaves; B1 controlled GREEN is not a substitute;
- final post-GUI B1/B2/IPC behavior regression before the canonical C1a runner;
- single-leaf selected counts, causal RED/UNREACHED/INVALID semantics and
  exact leaf-scoped Test/Worker paths.

The execution record must advance only through these checkpoints:

## Superseded blocker and selected technical decision

The approved Desktop deterministic Run has exactly two immutable sources,
members.csv and orders.csv, succeeds without a provider/model, and must retain
separate evidence under .xanthil/runs/<run_id>/.

Read-only source verification establishes that the current reusable contract:

- creates only schema 2.0 for analysis_kind analyst_assistant;
- requires sources.length === 1 and the exact canonical member-orders-v1
  fixture path/hash/bytes/version;
- requires non-empty Runtime/Adapter/provider/model provenance;
- is enforced by adapters/storage-local/local-analysis.ts through the current
  Product Core validator and fixture-specific evidence lifecycle.

Therefore the Desktop cannot use RunArtifactStore unchanged. The former Spec
correctly stopped rather than fake the old fixture/model, omit `.xanthil/runs`,
or substitute the SQLite index.

Source precheck evidence is
baseline-integrity-001/run-store-contract-precheck-001.json, 21124 bytes,
SHA-256
69767ed425e985f44a8238cc73f83f326b315540b284fd2aa6892dd3eca314fd.

Technical Decision Amendment 001 delegates that in-scope technical mapping,
and the Mini coordinator approves the minimal additive direction in principle.
The normative closed result is:

- Desktop manifest `schema_version: "3.0"`, exact two-source/no-model shape,
  exact file inventory, checked signed-64-bit/decimal-string arithmetic, and
  terminal `run.json` last;
- Product Core validation and `DesktopRunEvidenceStore` in the existing
  approved Desktop Core/Port/storage paths only;
- Desktop UUIDv7 Run IDs and native UUIDv4 for other new Desktop identities;
- current local-analysis store unchanged: writes 2.0, reads terminal 1.0/2.0,
  and rejects 3.0;
- current Console selected-directory reader unchanged: reads 1.0 only,
  returns `RUN_CONTRACT_UNSUPPORTED` for 3.0, and does not enumerate the root;
- terminal 3.0 publication is physical evidence linearization; the later
  matching SQLite Succeeded commit is Application visibility. A durable bundle
  without that commit stays immutable, unreferenced, unadopted, and invisible;
- no registry, dispatcher, migration, scan, repair, orphan adoption, historical
  rewrite, two-phase coordinator, or generic factory/store abstraction.

decision-brief.md records authority and compatibility; structure-decision.md
is the exact normative schema and publication contract.
